//<![CDATA[
     jQuery.noConflict();  
(function($) {  

// Init 
$(document).ready(function() {

//Sort random function
  function random(owlSelector){
    owlSelector.children().sort(function(){
        return Math.round(Math.random()) - 0.5;
    }).each(function(){
      $(this).appendTo(owlSelector);
    });
  }

// Main Carousel (homepage)
$("#owl-carousel").owlCarousel({
      autoPlay: 3000, //Set AutoPlay to 3 seconds
      autoplayHoverPause:true,
      navigation: true,
      beforeInit : function(elem){
      //Parameter elem pointing to $("#owl-carousel")
      random(elem);
    },
      singleItem:true,
      pagination:true
  });

// Carousel
$("#owl-demo").owlCarousel({
      autoPlay: 3000, //Set AutoPlay to 3 seconds
      autoplayHoverPause:true,
      navigation: true,
beforeInit : function(elem){
      //Parameter elem pointing to $("#owl-demo")
      random(elem);
    },
      items : 3,
      itemsDesktop : [1199,3],
      itemsDesktopSmall : [979,2],
      itemsTablet: [770,2],
      itemsMobile: [600,1]

  });

// Carousel 4 items
$("#owl-4").owlCarousel({
      autoPlay: false,
      autoplayHoverPause:true,
      navigation: true,

      //Parameter elem pointing to $("#owl-4")
      random(elem);
    },
      items : 4,
      itemsDesktop : [1199,3],
      itemsDesktopSmall : [979,3]

  });

    $('a[href^="*"]').on('click',function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();

            var target = this.hash,
            $target = $(target);

            $('html, body').stop().animate({
                'scrollTop': $target.offset().top
            }, 900, 'swing', function () {
                window.location.hash = target;
            });
        });
// tabs
        $('ul.tabs-nav > li:first-child').addClass('active');
        $('div.tab-content').hide();
        $('div.tab-content:first-child').show();
        
        $('ul.tabs-nav a').click(function() {
                var nav = $(this).parent().parent('ul.tabs-nav');
                if (!$(this).parent().hasClass('active')) {
                        $('> li', nav).removeClass('active');
                        $(this).parent().addClass('active');

                        var target = $(this).attr('href');
                        var container = $('div.tabs-container').has(target);
                        $('> .tab-content', container).hide();
                        $(target, container).fadeIn(); 
                }
                return false;
        });    
// hs-matrix

 $('#hs-slider-fullwidth-home').appendTo($('.flex-holder-home'));

    Best destinations in Europe.
</h1>
<h2 class="headline cc-m-header-inline-rte" data-action="header" data-name="header" id="cc-m-header-15173258324" style="line-height: 28px; min-height: 28px; text-align: center;">
    Explore the trendiest destinations
</h2>
<p>
     
</p>
<div class="divider gold">

<div id="cc-matrix-4492222024"><div class="j-module n j-text" id="cc-m-15314866724"><p style="text-align: justify;">
    In partnership with more than 300 European tourism offices including 80 sustainable tourism destinations, we invite you to discover the best of Europe. Do you want a <a href="/destinations/best-european-city-breaks/" title="City breaks">city break</a> or a refreshing break in the country? Can’t make up your mind between a holiday on one of the most beautiful <a href="/destinations/best-beach-destinations-in-europe/" title="Beaches">beaches</a> in Europe or a <a href="/destinations/best-ski-destinations-in-europe/" title="Skiing">family skiing holiday</a>, a <a href="/destinations/best-shopping-cities-in-europe/" title="Shopping">shopping trip</a> with friends or a <a href="/destinations/best-romantic-destinations-in-europe/" title="Romance">romantic getaway</a> with your lover? What about a <a href="/destinations/best-culinary-destinations-in-europe/" title="Gastronomy">gastronomic trip</a> or a <a href="/destinations/best-cultural-destinations-in-europe/" title="Culture">culture trip</a>?
</p>
<p style="text-align: justify;">
     
</p>
<p style="text-align: justify;">
    Discover the most beautiful destinations in Europe. Make your choice and plan your trip at the best price in just a few minutes.
